00032: def tube(parent,name,points,radius=.018,color=sage):
00033:     clean=[]
00034:     for p in points:
00035:         p=tuple(p)
00036:         if not clean or sum((a-b)**2 for a,b in zip(p,clean[-1]))>1e-12:
00037:             clean.append(p)
00038:     if len(clean)>1:
00039:         add(parent,name,sweep_tube(points=clean,radius=radius,color=color))

00044:             for t in [i/steps for i in range(steps+1)]]

00216: def spow(v,e):
00217:     return math.copysign(abs(v)**e,v)
00218: 
00219: def cushion(name,center,scale,material,ex=.34,ez=.55,rotation=(0,0,0),loose=False):
00220:     verts=[];faces=[]
00221:     nu,nv=64,28
00222:     for j in range(nv+1):
00223:         p=-math.pi/2+math.pi*j/nv
00224:         for i in range(nu):
00225:             t=2*math.pi*i/nu
00226:             x=scale[0]*spow(math.cos(p),ez)*spow(math.cos(t),ex)
00227:             y=scale[1]*spow(math.cos(p),ez)*spow(math.sin(t),ex)
00228:             z=scale[2]*spow(math.sin(p),ez)
00229:             if loose:
00230:                 # Small asymmetries and surface dimples keep the loose pillow soft.
00231:                 x*=1+.055*math.sin(t*5+p*7)
00232:                 z+=.018*math.sin(t*3)*math.cos(p)**2
00233:             verts.append((x,y,z))
00234:     for j in range(nv):
00235:         for i in range(nu):
00236:             a=j*nu+i;b=j*nu+(i+1)%nu
00237:             faces.append((a,b,b+nu,a+nu))
00238:     mesh=bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00239:     mesh.from_pydata(verts,[],faces);mesh.update()
00240:     obj=bpy.data.objects.new(name,mesh)
00241:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(obj)
00242:     obj.location=center;obj.rotation_euler=rotation
00243:     obj.data.materials.append(material)
00244:     for p in mesh.polygons:p.use_smooth=True
00245:     extra.append(obj)
00246:     return obj
